# Avalonia parity ledger

The single source of truth for "done" in the WPF -> Avalonia 12 port. One row per WPF window,
view, dialog, control, theme dictionary, shell partial and platform feature. The WPF head
(`ConditioningControlPanel/`) is the reference: a row is done when the Avalonia counterpart
matches it or is better, and is at least as fast.

Status, lowest to highest:
- `missing`: no Avalonia counterpart.
- `stub`: a counterpart exists but key behaviour is a placeholder, unwired or unavailable.
- `wired`: the counterpart implements the WPF behaviour as far as the source shows, and something in the
  Avalonia head opens or hosts it. Not yet proven. A surface nothing opens is `stub`, and so is one
  missing a WPF animation or motion effect.
- `verified`: proven on Linux by a headless test or render proof AND a Keincheck run on the live app
  (navigate, click, read state, screenshot), compared against the WPF reference. Evidence linked.
- `improved`: verified, and deliberately better than WPF; the reason is in avalonia-decisions.md.
- `blocked`: cannot progress yet; the blocker is in the notes. Revisit later.

`--render-all` (RenderProof) only proves a view draws; it never makes a row `verified` on its own.
Initial statuses were read from source on 2026-09-28 (stack tip #1765) and are not yet verified.

Starting totals (2026-09-28): 362 rows: 59 missing, 247 stub, 56 wired, 0 verified.

Notes carry what is stubbed or missing, platform-specific parts, existing evidence and, where it
matters, a performance note (startup, tab switch, list scroll, overlays, media).

| shell-clickthrough-overlays | `ConditioningControlPanel/Services/Notifications/OverlayService.cs`; `ConditioningControlPanel/Services/Compositor/LayeredCompositorHost.cs`; `ConditioningControlPanel/Overlays/` | `CCP.Avalonia/Platform/X11Overlay.cs`; `CCP.Avalonia/Views/Overlays/PinkFilterOverlay.cs`; `CCP.Avalonia/Views/Overlays/TintOverlayWindow.cs`; `CCP.Avalonia/Views/Chaos/ChaosOverlayWindow.axaml.cs`; `CCP.Avalonia/X11OverlayProbe.cs`; `CCP.Avalonia/OverlayCheck.cs` | stub | Mechanism (docs/avalonia-decisions.md, desktop overlays): X11 backend pinned on Linux (`Program.BuildAvaloniaApp` `UseX11()`; Wayland sessions run via XWayland), non-focus overlay = override-redirect ARGB window + empty XFixes input region, both set before `Show()` (`X11Overlay.SetOverrideRedirect(window, bounds)`, which sets override-redirect before any geometry so the WM cannot replay a stale size after map, + `SetClickThrough`). `--overlay-check` reads back per screen from the X server: XID exists and is unmapped before Show, then map_state=IsViewable, override_redirect=True, depth 32, 0 input rects, geometry == Screens bounds; 25/25 consecutive PASS live on this box (XDG_SESSION_TYPE=wayland, 3 screens; `overlay-check-loop-25.txt`) and in nested kwin; fails (exit 1) with override-redirect skipped. `--x11-probe` covers click-through toggle + restack. Evidence ~/ccp-port/evidence/avalonia-port/x11-override-redirect/. Flash images now open overlays through this (feat-flash; `X11Overlay.SetOpacity` added: compositor-side alpha, because re-rendering the overlays each fade frame on GLX/XWayland stalled the UI dispatcher ~1.7 s per burst). Windows (win32-overlay-shim): the same entry points route an "HWND" handle to `CCP.Avalonia/Platform/Win32Overlay.cs` (WS_EX_LAYERED|TOOLWINDOW + WS_EX_TRANSPARENT|NOACTIVATE toggled together, kept through Avalonia's style rebuilds via `Win32Properties.AddWindowStylesCallback`, HWND_TOPMOST, LWA_ALPHA opacity; WPF bits from `OverlayService.cs:1415`, `FlashService.cs:4614`/`:4838`); dispatch covered by `Tests/CCP.Avalonia.Tests/OverlayBackendDispatchTests.cs`; compiled for win-x64 only, never run here - verified only by the windows CI `--overlay-check` step (user32 read-back, SKIP without a desktop session). RestackAbove has no Win32 half. Risk: `SetOverrideRedirect` sizes by `DesktopScaling`, which on mixed-DPI Windows comes from the monitor the window was created on, so an overlay on a different-DPI screen may be mis-sized (the CI check has one screen). Still stub: tutorial spotlight hole/rect input remains unavailable. |

| shell-single-instance | `ConditioningControlPanel/App.xaml.cs` | `CCP.Avalonia/Program.cs`; `CCP.Avalonia/App.axaml.cs` | stub | Avalonia lifetime starts normally; no mutex, process forwarding, acknowledgment gate or zombie takeover. |
| shell-single-instance | `ConditioningControlPanel/App.xaml.cs` | `CCP.Avalonia/Program.cs`; `CCP.Avalonia/App.axaml.cs` | wired | `CCP.Avalonia/Platform/SingleInstance.cs`, claimed in `Program.cs` before the desktop lifetime: WPF's mutex name `ConditioningControlPanel_SingleInstance_Mutex` (installer AppMutex); a second launch asks the primary to show (ShowFromTray on its UI thread), waits up to WPF's 10 s for the ack and exits 0; no listener = WPF's 8 s mid-update wait, then exit; listener without ack = 3 s wait then run. Show/ack go over a named pipe (Unix socket on Linux) because WPF's named EventWaitHandles do not exist on Unix, so an Avalonia second launch cannot wake a running WPF build. A CCP_USERDATA_DIR sandbox suffixes the names so kc/tests never touch the user's app. `Tests/CCP.Avalonia.Tests/SingleInstanceTests.cs` (fail-proven: no show -> count 0; no ack -> second not null). Live: published linux-x64 build, second launch exit 0 in 0 s with first still up (~/ccp-port/evidence/avalonia-port/publish-profiles/shell-single-instance-live.txt). Not verified: no Keincheck run (Release publish). Launcher handoff (launcher-boot): the request carries the Core `LauncherHandoff` payload (length byte + UTF-8; 0 = bare relaunch) in place of WPF's handoff file, routed by `LauncherWindow.RouteHandoff`; `SingleInstanceTests.SecondLaunchHandsItsSurfaceToThePrimary` fail-proven; live ~/ccp-port/evidence/avalonia-port-launcher-boot/win-launcher-handoff.txt. On Unix .NET's named mutex is session-scoped, so a second launch from another login session (e.g. `setsid`) does not find the primary. Not ported: stale-instance kill (KillStaleInstances), "Open with CCP" file handoff, the startup-phase early ack. |

WPF CornerGifService rules: max 2 slots, primary screen, `CornerGifPlanner` placement + 40 DIP same-corner nudge, 400 ms realization stagger, 8x250 ms deferral while `DisplayChangeCoordinator.SpawnsSuppressed`, per-slot generation so a teardown cancels a queued show, art = slot pick -> pool spiral -> mod spiral -> shipped `spiral_corner.gif` (now linked), frames decoded off the UI thread at the overlay's pixel size, one frame swap per tick; override-redirect + empty input shape (click-through, no focus, no taskbar) like the other X11 overlays; no compositor -> not shown (logged). #709/#954/#958 restore sentinel (`logs/cornergif_restore.active`, armed while any overlay is up or pending): restored at ApplicationIdle; a surviving sentinel force-disables the slots with a 12 s warning toast (`corner_gif_force_disabled`, 9 languages). Panic: `PanicSurfaces` 'corner-gif' (after 'engine', WPF :1967) closes and cancels, settings untouched; shell close stops them. Lockdown: WPF applies none to this surface (no Lockdown check in CornerGifService/CornerGifWindow). No desktop screenshot: the session was locked (spectacle returned black). Differences from WPF (decisions log): Stop leaves bursts already on screen to finish (WPF closes them); closing the shell stops the schedule, closes open flashes and drops queued spawns (App MainWindow.Closed; overlay-text-families). 7.0.3 (port-fx): picks deal from the Core `ShuffleBag` (moved from WPF, #627 `0e7b2517a`) so a folder is walked in full before repeats, and the Random image count switch + Fewest slider roll `AppSettings.RollFlashImageCount` per burst (#658 `785bd5c69`); headless `FlashPickTests`. Card: `BoxFlashV2` (v2 pill + title) with `RowMotion` picker (Still, Drift & Bounce + v2 pill, Mix) and folded `ec0ad296a` (#1265) `RowDriftSpeed` slider 0.25-3 x (shown only while Drift is owned and picked or Mix), WPF BuildMotionPicker/UpdateDriftSpeedRow rules; ownership from `CCP.Avalonia/Platform/PrizeOwnership.cs` (row svc-prize-ownership: unowned in Release, WPF's DEBUG-only `CCP_PRIZE_GRANTS` honoured in Debug), so a Release user sees the box hidden and flashes Still, as WPF shows an account that bought nothing. Overlay: `FlashOverlay.BuildMotion` = WPF ResolveMotionStyle + FlashMotion.Create + FlashDriftSpeed x velocity (MotionLevel Off/Reduced honoured); one shared 16 ms dispatcher tick moves the override-redirect windows (a move, no re-render), real elapsed time from a TimeProvider, stops when the last drifting flash closes (expiry, Stop, panic via `App.StopDesktopOverlays` -> CloseAll). Headless `FlashDriftTests` (4, each fail-proven). Live (kc, sandbox, CCP_PRIZE_GRANTS=fx.flash.*, session locked): Studio > Flash, keyboard Down picks Drift & Bounce, Right x4 -> 3x, Test: X server geometry of 2 flashes changes every 100 ms sample (87/87 distinct, wall bounces visible) vs Still baseline 1 distinct; settings persisted FlashMotionStyle=1 FlashDriftSpeed=3.0; 0 binding errors (~/ccp-port/evidence/avalonia-port/rows-feat-flash/). Tests `TextOverlayTests.SubliminalCard_ClosesOnTime_EvenWithoutAFirstFrame` + `OverlayOpacity_IsNeverExactlyZero`, both fail-proven.
